import asyncio
import threading
import time

from langchain_core.runnables import (
    InMemoryCoalesceBackend,
    RunnableLambda,
    RunnablePassthrough,
)

PASS = []
FAIL = []
def check(name, cond):
    if cond: PASS.append(name)
    else:
        FAIL.append(name)
        print("FAIL:", name)

def test_astream_events_passthrough():
    async def f(x):
        await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
        return x * 2
    r = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    events = []
    async def main():
        async for ev in r.astream_events(1, version="v2"):
            events.append(ev["event"])
        return events
    events = asyncio.run(main())
    check("astream_events passthrough", any(e.startswith("on_chain_") for e in events))

def test_batch_return_exceptions():
    calls = []
    def f(x):
        time.sleep(0.05)
        calls.append(x)
        if x == 3:
            raise ValueError("bad 3")
        return x + 1
    r = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    out = r.batch([1, 1, 3, 3], return_exceptions=True)
    check("batch return_exceptions results", out[0] == 2 and out[1] == 2)
    check("batch return_exceptions errors", all(isinstance(o, ValueError) for o in out[2:]))
    check("batch return_exceptions coalesce", len(calls) == 2)  # 1 and 3

def test_batch_as_completed_return_exceptions():
    calls = []
    def f(x):
        time.sleep(0.05)
        calls.append(x)
        if x == 3:
            raise ValueError("bad 3")
        return x + 1
    r = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    results = list(r.batch_as_completed([1, 1, 3, 3], return_exceptions=True))
    vals = [v for _, v in results]
    check("bac return_exceptions results", 2 in vals and isinstance(vals[vals.index(2) + 1], int) if vals.count(2) == 2 else False)
    check("bac return_exceptions errors", sum(isinstance(v, ValueError) for v in vals) == 2)

def test_stats_during_execution():
    started = threading.Event()
    release = threading.Event()
    def f(x):
        started.set()
        release.wait(2)
        return x
    r = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    stats_snapshot = []
    t = threading.Thread(target=lambda: r.invoke(1))
    t.start()
    started.wait(1)
    stats_snapshot.append(r.coalesce_info())
    release.set()
    t.join(2)
    check("stats active during", stats_snapshot[0].active == 1)
    check("stats total during", stats_snapshot[0].total == 1)
    check("stats active after", r.coalesce_info().active == 0)

def test_backend_direct_api():
    b = InMemoryCoalesceBackend()
    check("register leader", b.register("k") is True)
    check("is_active", b.is_active("k") is True)
    check("register joiner", b.register("k") is False)
    check("stats coalesced", b.stats.coalesced == 1)
    check("stats total", b.stats.total == 2)
    b.complete("k", result="res")
    check("complete is_active", b.is_active("k") is False)
    check("join returns result", b.join("k") == "res")
    # fresh after complete
    check("register after complete fresh", b.register("k") is True)
    b.complete("k", result="res2")
    check("join fresh result", b.join("k") == "res2")

def test_backend_direct_async_api():
    b = InMemoryCoalesceBackend()
    async def main():
        a1 = await b.aregister("k")
        a2 = await b.aregister("k")
        active = await b.ais_active("k")
        await b.acomplete("k", result="res")
        joined = await b.ajoin("k")
        return a1, a2, active, joined, b.stats
    a1, a2, active, joined, stats = asyncio.run(main())
    check("async backend register", a1 is True and a2 is False)
    check("async backend active", active is True)
    check("async backend join", joined == "res")
    check("async backend stats", stats.coalesced == 1 and stats.total == 2)

def test_backend_async_join_wait():
    b = InMemoryCoalesceBackend()
    async def main():
        assert await b.aregister("k")
        result = []
        async def joiner():
            result.append(await b.ajoin("k"))
        t = asyncio.create_task(joiner())
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        check("async join pending", not result)
        await b.acomplete("k", result="done")
        await t
        check("async join wakes", result == ["done"])
    asyncio.run(main())

def test_stream_error_propagation():
    async def gen(x):
        yield "a"
        raise ValueError("boom")
    r = RunnableLambda(gen).with_coalesce()
    async def main():
        it1 = r.astream(1)
        it2 = r.astream(1)
        out1 = await anext(it1)
        joiner_task = asyncio.create_task(anext(it2))
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        try:
            rest1 = [c async for c in it1]
        except ValueError as e:
            rest1 = e
        try:
            out2 = await joiner_task
            rest2 = [c async for c in it2]
        except ValueError as e:
            out2 = e
            rest2 = []
        return out1, rest1, out2, rest2
    out1, rest1, out2, rest2 = asyncio.run(main())
    check("stream leader error", out1 == "a" and isinstance(rest1, ValueError))
    check("stream joiner error", isinstance(out2, ValueError))

def test_nested_dict_canonical():
    b = InMemoryCoalesceBackend()
    d1 = {"a": {"x": 1, "y": 2}, "b": [1, 2]}
    d2 = {"b": [1, 2], "a": {"y": 2, "x": 1}}
    check("nested dict canonical", b.register(d1) is True and b.register(d2) is False)

def test_with_coalesce_on_binding():
    def f(x, extra=0):
        return x + extra
    base = RunnableLambda(f).bind(extra=10)
    r = base.with_coalesce()
    out = r.invoke(1)
    check("with_coalesce on binding", out == 11)
    async def main():
        return await asyncio.gather(r.ainvoke(1), r.ainvoke(1))
    outs = asyncio.run(main())
    check("binding async coalesce", outs == [11, 11])

def test_clear_resets_and_allows_fresh():
    calls = []
    def f(x):
        calls.append(x)
        return x
    r = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    assert r.invoke(1) == 1
    r.coalesce_clear()
    stats = r.coalesce_info()
    check("clear resets", stats.total == 0 and stats.coalesced == 0 and stats.active == 0)
    assert r.invoke(1) == 1
    check("fresh after clear", len(calls) == 2)

def test_chain_composition():
    calls = []
    def f(x):
        time.sleep(0.1)
        calls.append(x)
        return x + 1
    inner = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    outer = RunnablePassthrough() | inner
    async def main():
        return await asyncio.gather(outer.ainvoke(5), outer.ainvoke(5))
    out = asyncio.run(main())
    check("chain coalesce", out == [6, 6] and len(calls) == 1)

def main():
    test_astream_events_passthrough()
    test_batch_return_exceptions()
    test_batch_as_completed_return_exceptions()
    test_stats_during_execution()
    test_backend_direct_api()
    test_backend_direct_async_api()
    test_backend_async_join_wait()
    test_stream_error_propagation()
    test_nested_dict_canonical()
    test_with_coalesce_on_binding()
    test_clear_resets_and_allows_fresh()
    test_chain_composition()
    print(f"\nPASS: {len(PASS)} FAIL: {len(FAIL)}")
    for f in FAIL:
        print(" -", f)
    if FAIL:
        raise SystemExit(1)

main()

    def with_coalesce(
        self,
        *,
        backend: CoalesceBackend | None = None,
    ) -> Runnable[Input, Output]:
        """Create a new `Runnable` that coalesces concurrent calls with identical inputs.

        When multiple callers invoke the returned `Runnable` with the same input
        concurrently, only one underlying execution runs and all callers receive the
        same result (or error). Coalescing applies to sync and async `invoke`,
        `stream`, `batch`, and `batch_as_completed`, sharing one backend so in-flight
        state is visible across methods.

        Args:
            backend: The coalescing backend to use. If `None`, a new
                `InMemoryCoalesceBackend` is created. Backends can be shared between
                multiple wrappers to coalesce across them.

        Returns:
            A new `Runnable` that coalesces concurrent calls.

        Example:
            ```python
            from langchain_core.runnables import RunnableLambda

            runnable = RunnableLambda(lambda x: expensive(x)).with_coalesce()

            # Concurrent calls with the same input run the underlying function once.
            ```
        """
        # Import locally to prevent circular import
        from langchain_core.runnables.coalesce import (  # noqa: PLC0415
            InMemoryCoalesceBackend,
            RunnableCoalesce,
        )

        if backend is None:
            backend = InMemoryCoalesceBackend()
        return RunnableCoalesce(bound=self, backend=backend)

    """ --- Helper methods for Subclasses --- """

    def _call_with_config(

    38	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    39	class CoalesceStats:
    40	    """Statistics about a coalescing backend."""
    41	
    42	    active: int
    43	    """Number of in-flight (not yet completed) unique executions."""
    44	
    45	    coalesced: int
    46	    """Cumulative number of calls that joined an in-flight execution."""
    47	
    48	    total: int
    49	    """Cumulative number of calls registered with the backend."""
    50	
    51	
    52	def _canonical_key(key: Any) -> Any:
    53	    """Produce a hashable key that is insensitive to dictionary key ordering.
    54	
    55	    The coalescing key is based only on the input value. Dictionaries are
    56	    canonicalized by sorting their items so that two dictionaries containing the
    57	    same key/value pairs in different orders map to the same key.
    58	    """
    59	    if isinstance(key, dict):
    60	        items = [(_canonical_key(k), _canonical_key(v)) for k, v in key.items()]
    61	        items.sort(key=repr)
    62	        return tuple(items)
    63	    if isinstance(key, (list, tuple)):
    64	        return tuple(_canonical_key(item) for item in key)
    65	    if isinstance(key, (set, frozenset)):
    66	        return frozenset(_canonical_key(item) for item in key)
    67	    try:
    68	        hash(key)
    69	    except TypeError:
    70	        # Fallback for unhashable custom objects. Using repr is deterministic for
    71	        # immutable values; dicts/lists/tuples/sets are handled above.
    72	        return ("__repr__", repr(key))
    73	    return key
    74	
    75	
    76	async def _aiter_single(value: Any) -> AsyncIterator[Any]:
    77	    """Return an async iterator that yields a single value."""
    78	    yield value
    79	
    80	
    81	class _SyncWaiter:
    82	    __slots__ = ("event", "result", "error", "cancelled")
    83	
    84	    def __init__(self) -> None:
    85	        self.event = threading.Event()
    86	        self.result: Any = None
    87	        self.error: BaseException | None = None
    88	        self.cancelled = False
    89	
    90	
    91	class _AsyncWaiter:
    92	    __slots__ = ("loop", "event", "result", "error", "cancelled")
    93	
    94	    def __init__(self, loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop) -> None:
    95	        self.loop = loop
    96	        self.event = asyncio.Event()
    97	        self.result: Any = None
    98	        self.error: BaseException | None = None
    99	        self.cancelled = False
   100

   104	        "completed",
   105	        "cancelled",

   108	        "sync_waiters",
   109	        "async_waiters",
   110	    )

    async for i in inputs:
        calls2.append(i)
        yield i * 3

    a = []
    b = []

        a.append(chunk)
    async for chunk in rag.astream(2):
        b.append(chunk)
    return a, b
a, b = asyncio.run(main())

@@ -2022,6 +2023,46 @@ class Runnable(ABC, Generic[Input, Output]):
             exception_key=exception_key,
         )
 
+    def with_coalesce(
+        self,
+        *,
+        backend: CoalesceBackend | None = None,
+    ) -> Runnable[Input, Output]:
+        """Create a new `Runnable` that coalesces concurrent calls with identical inputs.
+
+        When multiple callers invoke the returned `Runnable` with the same input
+        concurrently, only one underlying execution runs and all callers receive the
+        same result (or error). Coalescing applies to sync and async `invoke`,
+        `stream`, `batch`, and `batch_as_completed`, sharing one backend so in-flight
+        state is visible across methods.
+
+        Args:
+            backend: The coalescing backend to use. If `None`, a new
+                `InMemoryCoalesceBackend` is created. Backends can be shared between
+                multiple wrappers to coalesce across them.
+
+        Returns:
+            A new `Runnable` that coalesces concurrent calls.
+
+        Example:
+            ```python
+            from langchain_core.runnables import RunnableLambda
+
+            runnable = RunnableLambda(lambda x: expensive(x)).with_coalesce()
+
+            # Concurrent calls with the same input run the underlying function once.
+            ```
+        """
+        # Import locally to prevent circular import
+        from langchain_core.runnables.coalesce import (  # noqa: PLC0415
+            InMemoryCoalesceBackend,
+            RunnableCoalesce,
+        )
+
+        if backend is None:
+            backend = InMemoryCoalesceBackend()
+        return RunnableCoalesce(bound=self, backend=backend)
+
     """ --- Helper methods for Subclasses --- """
 
     def _call_with_config(

import asyncio, threading, time
from langchain_core.runnables import RunnableLambda

# batch item coalesces with concurrent invoke
calls = []
def f(x):
    time.sleep(0.3)
    calls.append(x)
    return x * 10

r = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
res = []
t = threading.Thread(target=lambda: res.append(r.invoke(1)))
t.start()
time.sleep(0.05)
batch_res = r.batch([1, 2])
t.join()
print("invoke+batch:", res, batch_res, "calls", calls, "stats", r.coalesce_info())

# async batch item coalesces with async invoke
calls2 = []
async def af(x):
    await asyncio.sleep(0.3)
    calls2.append(x)
    return x * 10

r2 = RunnableLambda(af).with_coalesce()
async def main():
    t = asyncio.create_task(r2.ainvoke(1))
    await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
    batch_res = await r2.abatch([1, 2])
    inv = await t
    return inv, batch_res
inv, batch_res = asyncio.run(main())
print("ainvoke+abatch:", inv, batch_res, "calls", calls2, "stats", r2.coalesce_info())

cd /app/libs/core && cat > tests/unit_tests/runnables/test_coalesce.py <<'PYEOF'
"""Tests for Runnable.with_coalesce and coalescing backends."""

import asyncio
import threading
import time
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Iterator

import pytest

from langchain_core.callbacks import BaseCallbackHandler
from langchain_core.runnables import (
    InMemoryCoalesceBackend,
    Runnable,
    RunnableLambda,
    RunnablePassthrough,
)
from langchain_core.runnables.coalesce import CoalesceBackend, CoalesceStats


def test_exports() -> None:
    from langchain_core.runnables import (  # noqa: PLC0415
        CoalesceBackend as ExportedBackend,
        CoalesceStats as ExportedStats,
        InMemoryCoalesceBackend as ExportedMemoryBackend,
    )

    assert ExportedBackend is CoalesceBackend
    assert ExportedStats is CoalesceStats
    assert ExportedMemoryBackend is InMemoryCoalesceBackend
    assert hasattr(Runnable, "with_coalesce")


async def test_async_invoke_coalesces() -> None:
    calls: list[int] = []

    async def slow(x: int) -> int:
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        calls.append(x)
        return x * 2

    runnable = RunnableLambda(slow).with_coalesce()
    results = await asyncio.gather(
        runnable.ainvoke(5), runnable.ainvoke(5), runnable.ainvoke(5)
    )
    assert results == [10, 10, 10]
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert runnable.coalesce_info() == CoalesceStats(active=0, coalesced=2, total=3)


def test_sync_invoke_coalesces() -> None:
    calls: list[int] = []
    lock = threading.Lock()

    def slow(x: int) -> int:
        time.sleep(0.1)
        with lock:
            calls.append(x)
        return x * 2

    runnable = RunnableLambda(slow).with_coalesce()
    results: list[int] = []

    def invoke() -> None:
        results.append(runnable.invoke(7))

    threads = [threading.Thread(target=invoke) for _ in range(3)]
    for thread in threads:
        thread.start()
    for thread in threads:
        thread.join()

    assert results == [14, 14, 14]
    assert len(calls) == 1


async def test_dict_key_ordering() -> None:
    calls: list[dict] = []

    def f(d: dict) -> int:
        calls.append(d)
        return sum(d.values())

    runnable = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    out = await asyncio.gather(
        runnable.ainvoke({"a": 1, "b": 2}),
        runnable.ainvoke({"b": 2, "a": 1}),
    )
    assert out == [3, 3]
    assert len(calls) == 1


def test_fresh_after_complete() -> None:
    calls: list[int] = []

    def f(x: int) -> int:
        calls.append(x)
        return x

    runnable = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    assert runnable.invoke(1) == 1
    assert runnable.invoke(1) == 1
    assert len(calls) == 2


def test_stream_join_replays_all_chunks() -> None:
    async def gen(x: int) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
        yield "a"
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        yield "b"
        yield "c"

    runnable = RunnableLambda(gen).with_coalesce()

    async def main() -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
        leader = runnable.astream(1)
        joiner = runnable.astream(1)
        first_leader = await anext(leader)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        joiner_first = asyncio.create_task(anext(joiner))
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        leader_rest = [chunk async for chunk in leader]
        first_joiner = await joiner_first
        joiner_rest = [chunk async for chunk in joiner]
        return [first_leader, *leader_rest], [first_joiner, *joiner_rest]

    leader_chunks, joiner_chunks = asyncio.run(main())
    assert leader_chunks == ["a", "b", "c"]
    assert joiner_chunks == ["a", "b", "c"]


def test_batch_coalesces_per_item() -> None:
    calls: list[int] = []

    def f(x: int) -> int:
        time.sleep(0.1)
        calls.append(x)
        return x + 1

    runnable = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    out = runnable.batch([1, 2, 1, 1, 2])
    assert out == [2, 3, 2, 2, 3]
    assert calls == [1, 2]


def test_batch_as_completed_duplicates_consecutive() -> None:
    calls: list[int] = []

    def f(x: int) -> int:
        time.sleep(0.1 * (x % 2))
        calls.append(x)
        return x * 10

    runnable = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    results = list(runnable.batch_as_completed([1, 1, 2]))
    indices = [i for i, _ in results]
    assert sorted(indices) == [0, 1, 2]
    assert calls.count(1) == 1
    assert abs(indices.index(0) - indices.index(1)) == 1


class CountingHandler(BaseCallbackHandler):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.starts = 0
        self.ends = 0

    def on_chain_start(self, *args: object, **kwargs: object) -> None:
        self.starts += 1

    def on_chain_end(self, *args: object, **kwargs: object) -> None:
        self.ends += 1


def test_joined_callers_fire_callbacks() -> None:
    async def f(x: int) -> int:
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        return x

    runnable = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    leader_handler = CountingHandler()
    joiner_handler = CountingHandler()

    async def main() -> None:
        await asyncio.gather(
            runnable.ainvoke(1, {"callbacks": [leader_handler]}),
            runnable.ainvoke(1, {"callbacks": [joiner_handler]}),
        )

    asyncio.run(main())
    assert joiner_handler.starts == 1
    assert joiner_handler.ends == 1
    assert leader_handler.starts >= 1
    assert leader_handler.ends >= 1


async def test_error_propagates_to_all() -> None:
    def f(x: int) -> int:
        raise ValueError("boom")

    runnable = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    results = await asyncio.gather(
        runnable.ainvoke(1), runnable.ainvoke(1), return_exceptions=True
    )
    assert all(isinstance(r, ValueError) for r in results)


def test_coalesce_clear_cancels_sync_waiter() -> None:
    started = threading.Event()
    release = threading.Event()

    def slow(x: int) -> int:
        started.set()
        release.wait(2)
        return x

    runnable = RunnableLambda(slow).with_coalesce()
    leader_result: list[object] = []
    joiner_result: list[object] = []

    def leader() -> None:
        try:
            leader_result.append(runnable.invoke(1))
        except BaseException as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            leader_result.append(e)

    def joiner() -> None:
        try:
            joiner_result.append(runnable.invoke(1))
        except BaseException as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            joiner_result.append(e)

    leader_thread = threading.Thread(target=leader)
    joiner_thread = threading.Thread(target=joiner)
    leader_thread.start()
    assert started.wait(1)
    joiner_thread.start()
    time.sleep(0.2)
    runnable.coalesce_clear()
    joiner_thread.join(1)
    assert len(joiner_result) == 1
    assert isinstance(joiner_result[0], asyncio.CancelledError)

    release.set()
    leader_thread.join(2)
    assert leader_result == [1]
    assert runnable.coalesce_info() == CoalesceStats(active=0, coalesced=0, total=0)


def test_shared_backend_coalesces_across_wrappers() -> None:
    calls: list[int] = []

    def f(x: int) -> int:
        time.sleep(0.1)
        calls.append(x)
        return x

    backend = InMemoryCoalesceBackend()
    runnable_1 = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce(backend=backend)
    runnable_2 = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce(backend=backend)
    results: list[int] = []

    threads = [
        threading.Thread(target=lambda: results.append(runnable_1.invoke(1))),
        threading.Thread(target=lambda: results.append(runnable_2.invoke(1))),
    ]
    for thread in threads:
        thread.start()
    for thread in threads:
        thread.join()

    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert results == [1, 1]


def test_separate_wrappers_coalesce_independently() -> None:
    calls: list[int] = []

    def f(x: int) -> int:
        time.sleep(0.1)
        calls.append(x)
        return x

    runnable_1 = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    runnable_2 = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    results: list[int] = []

    threads = [
        threading.Thread(target=lambda: results.append(runnable_1.invoke(1))),
        threading.Thread(target=lambda: results.append(runnable_2.invoke(1))),
    ]
    for thread in threads:
        thread.start()
    for thread in threads:
        thread.join()

    assert len(calls) == 2


def test_graph_delegation() -> None:
    runnable = RunnableLambda(lambda x: x + 1).with_coalesce()
    assert runnable.get_graph() is not None


def test_transform_passes_through() -> None:
    calls: list[int] = []

    def f(x: int) -> int:
        calls.append(x)
        return x

    runnable = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    assert list(runnable.transform(iter([5]))) == [5]
    assert len(calls) == 1


def test_chain_composition_coalesces() -> None:
    calls: list[int] = []

    def f(x: int) -> int:
        time.sleep(0.1)
        calls.append(x)
        return x + 1

    chain = (RunnablePassthrough() | RunnableLambda(f)).with_coalesce()

    async def main() -> list[int]:
        return await asyncio.gather(chain.ainvoke(5), chain.ainvoke(5))

    assert asyncio.run(main()) == [6, 6]
    assert len(calls) == 1


def test_backend_direct_api() -> None:
    backend = InMemoryCoalesceBackend()
    assert backend.register("key") is True
    assert backend.is_active("key") is True
    assert backend.register("key") is False
    assert backend.stats == CoalesceStats(active=1, coalesced=1, total=2)
    backend.complete("key", result="result")
    assert backend.is_active("key") is False
    assert backend.join("key") == "result"
    assert backend.register("key") is True
    backend.complete("key", error=ValueError("bad"))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        backend.join("key")


async def test_backend_async_api() -> None:
    backend = InMemoryCoalesceBackend()
    assert await backend.aregister("key") is True
    assert await backend.ais_active("key") is True
    assert await backend.aregister("key") is False

    async def joiner() -> str:
        return await backend.ajoin("key")

    task = asyncio.create_task(joiner())
    await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
    await backend.acomplete("key", result="result")
    assert await task == "result"
    assert await backend.ais_active("key") is False

import asyncio, time, threading
from langchain_core.callbacks import BaseCallbackHandler
from langchain_core.runnables import RunnableLambda

class H(BaseCallbackHandler):
    def __init__(self):
        self.starts = 0
        self.ends = 0
        self.errors = 0
    def on_chain_start(self, *a, **k): self.starts += 1
    def on_chain_end(self, *a, **k): self.ends += 1
    def on_chain_error(self, *a, **k): self.errors += 1

# stream joiner callbacks
async def test_stream_callbacks():
    async def gen(x):
        yield "a"
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        yield "b"
    r = RunnableLambda(gen).with_coalesce()
    h1, h2 = H(), H()
    async def main():
        it1 = r.astream(1, {"callbacks": [h1]})
        it2 = r.astream(1, {"callbacks": [h2]})
        first = await anext(it1)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        t = asyncio.create_task(anext(it2))
        rest1 = [c async for c in it1]
        second = await t
        rest2 = [c async for c in it2]
        return [first]+rest1, [second]+rest2
    r1, r2 = asyncio.run(main())
    print("stream callbacks:", h1.starts, h1.ends, h2.starts, h2.ends, r1, r2)
    assert h2.starts == 1 and h2.ends == 1

# abatch with max_concurrency=1 and duplicates
def test_abatch_max_concurrency():
    calls = []
    async def f(x):
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        calls.append(x)
        return x
    r = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    async def main():
        return await r.abatch([1, 1, 2], {"max_concurrency": 1})
    out = asyncio.run(main())
    print("abatch max_concurrency:", out, calls)
    assert out == [1, 1, 2] and len(calls) == 2

# abatch_as_completed max_concurrency
def test_abac_max_concurrency():
    calls = []
    async def f(x):
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        calls.append(x)
        return x
    r = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    async def main():
        out = []
        async for item in r.abatch_as_completed([1,1,2], {"max_concurrency": 1}):
            out.append(item)
        return out
    out = asyncio.run(main())
    idxs = [i for i,_ in out]
    print("abac max_concurrency:", out, calls)
    assert sorted(i for i,_ in out) == [0,1,2]
    assert abs(idxs.index(0)-idxs.index(1)) == 1
    assert len(calls) == 2

# sync batch_as_completed max_concurrency
def test_bac_max_concurrency():
    calls = []
    def f(x):
        time.sleep(0.05)
        calls.append(x)
        return x
    r = RunnableLambda(f).with_coalesce()
    out = list(r.batch_as_completed([1,1,2], {"max_concurrency": 1}))
    idxs = [i for i,_ in out]
    print("bac max_concurrency:", out, calls)
    assert abs(idxs.index(0)-idxs.index(1)) == 1
    assert len(calls) == 2

test_stream_callbacks()
test_abatch_max_concurrency()
test_abac_max_concurrency()
test_bac_max_concurrency()
print("ALL OK")

p = 'langchain_core/runnables/coalesce.py'
s = open(p).read()
old = '''        with self._lock:
            for execution in self._executions.values():
                execution.cancelled = True
                for waiter in execution.sync_waiters:
                    waiter.cancelled = True
                    waiter.event.set()
                for waiter in execution.async_waiters:
                    waiter.cancelled = True
                    try:
                        waiter.loop.call_soon_threadsafe(waiter.event.set)
                    except RuntimeError:
                        # The waiter's event loop is already closed.
                        pass
                execution.sync_waiters = []
                execution.async_waiters = []
            self._executions.clear()
            self._active = 0
            self._coalesced = 0
            self._total = 0'''
new = '''        with self._lock:
            for execution in self._executions.values():
                execution.cancelled = True
                for waiter in execution.sync_waiters:
                    waiter.cancelled = True
                    waiter.event.set()
                for waiter in execution.async_waiters:
                    waiter.cancelled = True
                    try:
                        waiter.loop.call_soon_threadsafe(waiter.event.set)
                    except RuntimeError:
                        # The waiter's event loop is already closed.
                        pass
                execution.sync_waiters = []
                execution.async_waiters = []
            # Keep the cancelled executions so that late joiners raise
            # `asyncio.CancelledError` instead of `KeyError`. The next `register`
            # for a key treats a cancelled execution as fresh and replaces it.
            self._active = 0
            self._coalesced = 0
            self._total = 0'''
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new, 1)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("updated clear")

import time
from langchain_core.runnables import RunnableLambda

calls = []
def f(x):
    time.sleep(0.05)
    calls.append(x)
    if x == 3:
        raise ValueError("bad")
    return x

print(results)
idxs = [i for i, _ in results]
# 0 and 1 consecutive, 2 and 3 consecutive
assert abs(idxs.index(0) - idxs.index(1)) == 1
assert abs(idxs.index(2) - idxs.index(3)) == 1
print("OK")

# Check for unused imports in coalesce.py (rough)
import ast, sys
tree = ast.parse(open('langchain_core/runnables/coalesce.py').read())
imports = []
for node in ast.walk(tree):
    if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
        for a in node.names:
            imports.append((a.asname or a.name, node.lineno))
    elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):
        for a in node.names:
            imports.append((a.asname or a.name.split('.')[0], node.lineno))
names = set()
for node in ast.walk(tree):
    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
        names.add(node.id)
    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):
        pass
for name, lineno in imports:
    if name not in names and name != 'annotations':
        print("possibly unused:", name, lineno)

STEP 133 CALL 1 RETURN 128
COMMAND:
cd /app && git commit -m "feat(core): add Runnable.with_coalesce for request coalescing

Add a with_coalesce method to Runnable that wraps it with request
coalescing. When multiple callers invoke with the same input
concurrently, only one underlying execution runs and all callers receive
the same result (or error).

New types CoalesceBackend, CoalesceStats, and InMemoryCoalesceBackend
live in langchain_core.runnables.coalesce and are exported from
langchain_core.runnables. The InMemory backend is thread-safe and
supports both synchronous and asynchronous waiting.

Coalescing applies to sync/async invoke, stream, batch, and
batch-as-completed, while transform, atransform, and event streaming
pass through transparently. Batch methods coalesce per item and preserve
positional order; batch-as-completed yields coalesced duplicates
consecutively. Joined callers fire chain-start and chain-end callbacks.
The wrapper exposes coalesce_info() and coalesce_clear()." && git log --oneline -3
OUTPUT:

  git config --global user.name "Your Name"

to set your account's default identity.

    @overload
    def batch_as_completed(
        self,
        inputs: Sequence[Input],
        config: RunnableConfig | Sequence[RunnableConfig] | None = None,
        *,
        return_exceptions: Literal[True],
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> Iterator[tuple[int, Output | Exception]]: ...

    @override
    def batch_as_completed(
        self,
        inputs: Sequence[Input],
        config: RunnableConfig | Sequence[RunnableConfig] | None = None,
        *,
        return_exceptions: bool = False,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> Iterator[tuple[int, Output | Exception]]:
        if not inputs:
            return
        configs = get_config_list(config, len(inputs))
        index_keys: list[Any] = []
        groups: dict[Any, list[int]] = {}
        for i, input_ in enumerate(inputs):
            ckey = _canonical_key(input_)
            index_keys.append(ckey)
            groups.setdefault(ckey, []).append(i)
        pending = {ckey: len(indices) for ckey, indices in groups.items()}
        buffered: dict[Any, list[tuple[int, Output | Exception]]] = {}

        def run_one(
            i: int, input_: Input, cfg: RunnableConfig
        ) -> tuple[int, Output | Exception]:
            if return_exceptions:
                try:
                    return i, self.invoke(input_, cfg, **kwargs)
                except Exception as e:
                    return i, e
            return i, self.invoke(input_, cfg, **kwargs)

        if len(inputs) == 1:
            yield run_one(0, inputs[0], configs[0])
            return

        with get_executor_for_config(configs[0]) as executor:
            futures = {
                executor.submit(run_one, i, input_, cfg)
                for i, (input_, cfg) in enumerate(zip(inputs, configs, strict=False))
            }
            try:
                while futures:
                    done, futures = wait(futures, return_when=FIRST_COMPLETED)
                    for future in done:
                        i, out = future.result()
                        ckey = index_keys[i]
                        buffered.setdefault(ckey, []).append((i, out))
                        pending[ckey] -= 1
                        if pending[ckey] == 0:
                            for item in buffered[ckey]:
                                yield item
                            buffered[ckey].clear()
            finally:
                for future in futures:
                    future.cancel()

    def abatch_as_completed(
        self,

        config: RunnableConfig | Sequence[RunnableConfig] | None = None,

    @override

        self,

        config: RunnableConfig | Sequence[RunnableConfig] | None = None,
        *,
        return_exceptions: bool = False,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> AsyncIterator[tuple[int, Output | Exception]]:
        if not inputs:
            return
        configs = get_config_list(config, len(inputs))
        index_keys: list[Any] = []
        groups: dict[Any, list[int]] = {}
        for i, input_ in enumerate(inputs):
            ckey = _canonical_key(input_)
            index_keys.append(ckey)
            groups.setdefault(ckey, []).append(i)
        pending = {ckey: len(indices) for ckey, indices in groups.items()}
        buffered: dict[Any, list[tuple[int, Output | Exception]]] = {}

        async def run_one(
            i: int, input_: Input, cfg: RunnableConfig
        ) -> tuple[int, Output | Exception]:
            if return_exceptions:
                try:
                    return i, await self.ainvoke(input_, cfg, **kwargs)
                except Exception as e:
                    return i, e
            return i, await self.ainvoke(input_, cfg, **kwargs)

        if len(inputs) == 1:
            yield await run_one(0, inputs[0], configs[0])
            return

        max_concurrency = configs[0].get("max_concurrency") if configs else None
        semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(max_concurrency) if max_concurrency else None
        coros = [
            gated_coro(semaphore, run_one(i, input_, cfg))
            if semaphore
            else run_one(i, input_, cfg)
            for i, (input_, cfg) in enumerate(zip(inputs, configs, strict=False))
        ]
        for coro in asyncio.as_completed(coros):
            i, out = await coro
            ckey = index_keys[i]
            buffered.setdefault(ckey, []).append((i, out))
            pending[ckey] -= 1
            if pending[ckey] == 0:
                for item in buffered[ckey]:
                    yield item
                buffered[ckey].clear()

    def _stream_transformer(
        self,
        inputs: Iterator[Input],
        run_manager: "CallbackManagerForChainRun",
        config: RunnableConfig,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> Iterator[Output]:
        input_ = next(inputs, None)
        if input_ is None:
            return
        if self.backend.register(input_):
            chunks: list[Output] = []
            try:
                for chunk in self.bound.stream(input_, config, **kwargs):
                    chunks.append(chunk)
                    yield chunk
            except GeneratorExit:
                raise
            except BaseException as e:
                self.backend.complete(input_, error=e)
                raise
            self.backend.complete(input_, result=chunks)
        else:
            result = self.backend.join(input_)
            for chunk in cast("list[Output]", result):
                yield chunk

    @override
    def stream(
        self,
        input: Input,
        config: RunnableConfig | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> Iterator[Output]:
        yield from self._transform_stream_with_config(
            iter([input]),
            self._stream_transformer,
            ensure_config(config),
            **kwargs,
        )

    async def _astream_transformer(
        self,
        inputs: AsyncIterator[Input],
        run_manager: "AsyncCallbackManagerForChainRun",
        config: RunnableConfig,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> AsyncIterator[Output]:
        input_ = await anext(inputs, None)
        if input_ is None:
            return
        if await self.backend.aregister(input_):
            chunks: list[Output] = []
            try:
                async for chunk in self.bound.astream(input_, config, **kwargs):
                    chunks.append(chunk)
                    yield chunk
            except GeneratorExit:
                raise
            except BaseException as e:
                await self.backend.acomplete(input_, error=e)
                raise
            await self.backend.acomplete(input_, result=chunks)
        else:
            result = await self.backend.ajoin(input_)
            for chunk in cast("list[Output]", result):
                yield chunk

            self._astream_transformer,
            ensure_config(config),
            **kwargs,

    def _batch(
        self,
        inputs: list[Input],
        run_manager: list["CallbackManagerForChainRun"],
        config: list[RunnableConfig],
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> list[Output | Exception]:
        def run_one(input_: Input, cfg: RunnableConfig) -> Output | Exception:

                except BaseException as e:
                    self.backend.complete(input_, error=e)
                    return e

                return out

                    return self.backend.join(input_)
                except BaseException as e:
                    return e
